CAS write failure (disk full) io::Error on put In-flight CAS writes fail Free disk; rebuild CasWriteFailed; disk metrics
Working-dir create failure mkdir→EACCES Action cannot run Fix base-dir permissions WorkingDirCreateFailed
Partial output after crash Output exists, NodeState not persisted Under-build risk Missing state → node Dirty, re-exec (persist crash-consistency) WARN IncrementalStateInconsistent
Hard-link quota exhaustion EMLINK Cache-hit materialize fails Copy fallback; GC old blobs ExecError::LinkFailed

6.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: exec_duration_ms (histogram by action-key prefix), exec_cache_hit_rate, exec_output_bytes, exec_stderr_bytes, exec_cancelled_count; tracing spans exec→capture/cas_put/materialize.
  • Logging: DEBUG working-dir path + env keys (not values) + output paths; ERROR missing outputs / non-zero exits with truncated stderr.
  • ibg.toml [exec] working_dir_base (default $IBGROOT/.ibg/exec/, tmpfs recommended for speed); co-locate with CAS to guarantee hard-linking (else EXDEV copy overhead, documented).
  • ExecOutcome field additions bump the ibg-persist schema version → full re-eval on first run, never a silent stale reuse; Executor trait is pub so third-party (remote/container) executors plug in without a crate fork.

19. Phase 7 — Parallel scheduler — dependency-aware work-stealing, ready-queue, resource constraints (job slots), critical-path scheduling, deadlock/backpressure handling

7.1 Objectives

Goal Constraint
Execute independent ready actions in parallel up to --jobs N N ≤ logical CPUs×2; default = logical CPUs
Dependency-aware dispatch: run only when all static deps Clean Dynamic deps (Phase 8) treated as static once registered
Work-stealing (idle threads steal from overloaded queues) + critical-path ordering (longest-remaining-path first) Stealing unit is one action, not a sub-DAG; CP weight = sum of estimated durations on the longest remaining chain
Backpressure caps in-flight actions when CAS/exec saturate Bounded job_slots: Arc<Semaphore>
Deadlock detection: scheduler-state cycle (distinct from graph cycle) → error Graph is DAG-verified in Phase 2; scheduler deadlock is a bug, caught via timeout + waiter audit

Scope: ibg-sched crate, integrates ibg-exec::Executor, ibg-engine node-state queries, ibg-telemetry. Must stay correct under arbitrary action failure (dependents Skipped, never deadlocked) and support whole-build cancellation (Ctrl-C) with graceful in-flight kill.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Modules: scheduler.rs (WorkStealingScheduler orchestrator), ready_queue.rs (ReadyQueue, CP-weight priority queue), job_slot.rs (semaphore wrapper), dep_tracker.rs (DepTracker, in-degree counters + completion callbacks), critical_path.rs (CriticalPathEstimator), worker.rs (steal+dispatch loop), backpressure.rs (BackpressureController), error.rs (SchedError).

Core structures: WorkStealingScheduler{ready_queue:Arc<Mutex<ReadyQueue>>, dep_tracker:Arc<DepTracker>, job_slots:Arc<Semaphore>, workers:Vec<JoinHandle<()>>, cancel:CancellationToken, executor:Arc<dyn Executor>, telemetry}; ReadyQueue{heap:BinaryHeap<ReadyEntry>} max-heap by cp_weight; ReadyEntry{node_id,cp_weight}; DepTracker{in_degree:DashMap<NodeId,AtomicU32>, rev_deps:DashMap<NodeId,Vec<NodeId>>}.

Build execution loop (P12/ADR-08): CriticalPathEstimator computes per-node CP weight via a reverse-topo pass; DepTracker::init sets each node's in-degree to its unfinished-dep count; nodes with in-degree 0 seed the ready queue; W worker tasks (W=min(jobs, tokio worker threads)) each acquire a job slot, pop the highest-cp_weight ready entry (parking on an empty queue), dispatch to Executor::run, and on completion release the slot and call dep_tracker.complete(node_id,result) — which decrements every rev-dep's in-degree, pushing any that reach 0, and on Failed marks all transitive dependents Skipped via a BFS over rev_deps. The build finishes when the ready queue is empty, in-flight count is 0, and no pending nodes remain.

Work stealing: tokio's multi-thread runtime steals across tasks natively over the shared Mutex<BinaryHeap> queue; a sched-deque feature flag swaps in per-worker crossbeam_deque::Worker<ReadyEntry> + Stealer refs for true per-worker deques at high concurrency.

Critical-path estimator: cp_weight[n] = estimated_duration[n] + max(cp_weight[dep] for dep in rev_deps[n]), estimated_duration sourced from last-build timing in ibg-persist (fallback = 1, uniform); recomputed once at schedule-init, not mid-build.

Backpressure: BackpressureController monitors CAS/exec queue depth; above backpressure_threshold (default 2×jobs) it parks new completions via a tokio::sync::watch broadcast until the queue drains.

Deadlock detection: a watchdog fires SchedError::DeadlockSuspected (logging every in-flight node + its awaited deps) if the ready queue is empty with in_flight>0 for longer than deadlock_timeout (default 60s); true graph cycles are excluded by the Phase-2 check, so this always signals an internal scheduler bug (InternalDeadlock).

Observability: ActionQueued{node_id,cp_weight,queue_depth}, ActionStarted{node_id,worker_id,ts}, ActionCompleted{node_id,outcome,wait_ms,exec_ms}, gauges sched_queue_depth/sched_in_flight.

Tests: proptest random DAGs (every non-failed node executes exactly once, in dependency order); 10K-node/8-worker stress test (no deadlock within 30s); failure-propagation test (one failure → all transitive dependents Skipped, none Running).

7.3 Deliverable

  • ibg-sched drives a 10K-synthetic-action build to completion; build_critical_path_ms visible in telemetry; ibg-bench::sched_throughput ≥2000 actions/s at 8 workers/10K trivial actions.
  • Queue-depth gauge visible in the metrics sink, steal events in TRACE logs; Ctrl-C cancellation stops all workers and exits within 2s with in-flight actions killed.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Pass Condition
Dependency order + parallelism No dispatch before all deps Clean (property test); 8-worker/1K-independent-action build ≤1.5× single-action time
Failed-node propagation All transitive dependents Skipped, never Running/Pending
Deadlock detection + job-slot enforcement Injected hang → DeadlockSuspected within 60s; never >N actions Running simultaneously
Critical-path priority + cancellation Diamond DAG's heavier branch dispatched first; build cancelled within 2s of cancel()
cargo test -p ibg-sched All tests green

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Outcome
sched/linear_chain.toml N=10 linear chain Dispatched strictly in order 0→9
sched/diamond.toml Diamond DAG Both branches dispatched with timestamp overlap
sched/failure_propagation.toml Action 3 fails BuildResult.skipped == [4,5,6]
sched/job_limit.toml 100 independent actions, jobs=2 Peak in-flight ≤2 at all times
sched/cancel.toml Cancel after first completion BuildResult contains Cancelled
sched/deadlock.toml MockExecutor hangs all in-flight DeadlockSuspected within timeout
sched/cp_priority.toml Two ready actions, one on critical path CP action dispatched first

Malformed: jobs=0 → InvalidJobCount. Replay: same DAG twice → deterministic dispatch order (fixed-seed property test).

7.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Recovery
Mutex contention on ReadyQueue Throughput collapses at 32+ workers Single Mutex<BinaryHeap> hot Shard by priority bucket or switch to crossbeam_deque
False deadlock detection Watchdog fires on a legitimately long action deadlock_timeout too short Set ≥ max expected action duration, configurable
In-degree undercount / negative in-degree Dispatch before deps ready / underflow on duplicate completion Non-atomic dep-completion check / same node completed twice fetch_sub+check==0 atomically; assert old_val>0 before sub
Skipped-node propagation stalls Build hangs, in_degree>0 on pending nodes BFS over rev_deps stops at first level Must be transitive; use a worklist queue
Worker starvation sched_in_flight shows imbalance tokio work-stealing not exposing a custom queue Enable sched-deque, explicit crossbeam steal
Cancel races in-flight counter Build reports done while actions still run cancel checked only at task start Check token inside Executor::run at I/O await points

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • BinaryHeap push reorder is O(log N) (~17 ops at N=100K, acceptable); DashMap rev_deps lookup contention mitigated by raising shard count from 16 to 64 on large graphs.
  • Semaphore acquire is O(1), non-issue ≤128 slots; CP-weight recomputation is O(V+E) at build start, cached in ibg-persist and invalidated on graph change.
  • Spawn W persistent worker tasks rather than one tokio::spawn per action; store Arc<Vec<NodeId>> (not Vec<NodeId>) in rev_deps to avoid clone-on-read; in-degree map is ~40 bytes/node (40 MB at 1M nodes, acceptable).

7.8 Edge Cases

  • Single node / all-independent: in-degree 0, trivially queued / all queued at init, throughput = N/jobs × action_time; all-linear-chain (worst case): parallelism=1 regardless of jobs, critical path = N × action_time.
  • jobs=1: degenerates to sequential, must still work, no deadlock possible; failure at root: all others Skipped, built=0,skipped=N-1.
  • Dynamic dep added mid-build: DepTracker::add_dynamic_dep() increments the suspended node's in-degree (Phase 8); action timeout: ExecContext::action_timeout kills the child on expiry, propagates as ActionFailed.
  • Empty build request: dispatches nothing, BuildResult::default() immediately; duplicate completion signal: InternalError with a debug-build assertion.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery Operator Signal
Worker task panic tokio JoinError That worker's work stops Spawn replacement; investigate WorkerPanic; stack trace
ReadyQueue lock poisoned Mutex::lock() PoisonError Scheduler halts Restart process InternalLockPoisoned
DepTracker corruption In-degree negative, assert fires Node dispatched out of order Full re-eval next invocation ERROR with node_id + in-degree
Semaphore permit leak No new dispatches Starvation Watchdog deadlock timeout; restart sched_in_flight stuck at N
CP weight overflow u64 wraps on extreme chains Wrong (but finite) priority saturating_add; no correctness issue WARN CriticalPathOverflow

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: sched_queue_depth/sched_in_flight (gauges), sched_wait_ms (per-node histogram), sched_steal_count, build_critical_path_ms; tracing scheduler span with per-action cp_weight child spans.
  • Logging: INFO build start/end + job-slot count, DEBUG per-dispatch cp_weight, TRACE steal events/in-degree updates.
  • Tuning (ibg.toml): sched.jobs (default nCPU), sched.deadlock_timeout_s (60), sched.backpressure_threshold (2×jobs), sched.use_deque (false); alert on sched_queue_depth > 2×jobs for >30s or sched_in_flight==jobs for >5min (executor bottleneck).
  • Scheduler is stateless between builds (no migration needed, only ibg-persist timing affects CP weights); WorkStealingScheduler::new(executor,jobs,telemetry,cancel) is public, Executor substitutable (tests + Phase 11 remote exec).

20. Phase 8 — Dynamic dependencies — deps discovered during execution (e.g. #include scanning, monadic deps), graph mutation mid-build, restarting/suspending nodes, dynamic-cycle detection

8.1 Objectives

Goal Constraint
Support deps discovered during exec (C/C++ .d files, Makefile depfiles, custom scanners) Discovered deps are DynamicDep{discovered_at:Revision} edges in ibg-graph
Mid-build graph mutation: add Edge{kind:Dynamic} without tearing down the scheduler Append-only during a build; no edge removal mid-build
Suspend + restart nodes whose deps were unknown at dispatch Suspended node releases its job slot; re-queued once the dep resolves
Persist dynamic edges across invocations Persisted deps become static in the next build's initial graph
Detect dynamic-dep cycles introduced mid-build Cycle check on edge addition; error if formed
Monadic build style (Shake): eval requests deps, awaits, continues ibg-engine eval is re-entrant/resumable via async await points

Scope: ibg-graph (edge mutation), ibg-engine (suspend/resume), ibg-sched (re-queue), ibg-persist (storage), ibg-exec (depfile parsing). Correctness invariant: a node re-executed because a dynamic dep changed must still satisfy no-under-build; cycle detection must never deadlock the scheduler while holding graph locks.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Modules: ibg-graph/src/dynamic.rs (DynamicEdgeRegistry), ibg-engine/src/suspend.rs (SuspendSet), ibg-sched/src/dynamic.rs (DynDepNotifier), ibg-exec/src/depfile.rs (DepfileParser), ibg-persist/src/dynamic.rs (edge storage).

DynamicEdgeRegistry (ibg-graph): {edges:RwLock<HashMap<NodeId,Vec<DynamicDep>>> (from→deps), rev:RwLock<HashMap<NodeId,Vec<NodeId>>> (dep→dependents)}; add_edge(from,dep,rev)->Result<(),CycleError>, dependents_of(dep)->Vec<NodeId>. Cycle detection runs DFS from dep over the static+dynamic graph before insert (if from is reachable → CycleError); the graph is read-locked and the registry write-locked only after the check passes, bounded by a visited set (max depth = graph diameter).

SuspendSet (ibg-engine, the "suspending rebuilder" of P5 applied to monadic/self-tracking deps per P2): {waiting:DashMap<NodeId,SuspendRecord{awaiting_deps:Vec<NodeId>, waker:Option<Waker>}>}. When eval hits an unresolved dynamic dep D for node N, it inserts SuspendRecord{[D],waker}; when D completes, DynDepNotifier::notify(D) looks up every node awaiting D and wakes it. eval_node is async: at the await point it checks whether the dep is Clean, yields via a Waker-based future if not, and on wake re-checks and either proceeds or re-suspends; the job slot is released on first suspend and re-acquired on resume.

Depfile parsing (ibg-exec/src/depfile.rs): post-exec, if Action.depfile:Option<PathBuf> is set, the Make-format .d file (target: dep1 dep2 \ dep3) is parsed into paths, each resolved relative to the working dir into a NodeKey::Source, and registered via DynamicEdgeRegistry::add_edge(action_node,source_node,current_revision).

Persistence (ibg-persist/src/dynamic.rs): table dynamic_edges(from_node_id,dep_node_id,discovered_at); loaded at build start (promoted to Edge{kind:Dynamic}), appended at build end with only this build's new edges; a schema bump follows any table-structure change.

In-degree adjustment (ibg-sched): DepTracker::add_dynamic_dep(node,dep) atomically increments node's in-degree; dep's completion still drives the normal complete() decrement across all rev-deps, dynamic or static.

Observability: DynamicDepDiscovered{from_node,dep_node,source:Depfile|Monadic}, NodeSuspended{node_id,awaiting}, NodeResumed{node_id,dep_node}, counter dynamic_dep_count.

Tests: unit (add_edge with/without a cycle), integration (depfile output → second build with a modified dep rebuilds correctly), proptest (random dynamic-edge additions never admit a true cycle), replay (kill mid-build with dynamic deps, restart, assert no under-build).

8.3 Deliverable

  • Synthetic C-like project build (header deps via depfile): first build runs everything, a header touch triggers a second build that rebuilds only dependents; telemetry shows DynamicDepDiscovered/NodeSuspended/NodeResumed.
  • ibg-persist stores dynamic edges (ibg-cli graph --show-dynamic displays them); a cycle-forming dep reports DynamicCycle; BuildResult.dynamic_deps_discovered is visible in the build summary.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Pass Condition
Depfile parsed + registered Post-exec dynamic_edges rows + graph edges appear
Incremental rebuild correctness Touching a depfile-declared source rebuilds only affected actions; unrelated touch dispatches zero
Cycle detection Cycle-forming dynamic edge → DynamicCycle, build fails
Suspend/resume + job-slot release Exactly one resume per resolved dep, no double-dispatch; sched_in_flight drops on suspend, rises on resume
Persist + reload Dynamic edges survive a restart; first action-set unchanged
Large-scale dyndep (dyndep/large.toml) 1000 actions × 3 dynamic deps each register within 5 s, zero deadlocks
cargo test -p ibg-graph -p ibg-engine -p ibg-sched 100% pass, all dynamic-dep tests green

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
dyndep/depfile_basic.toml Action writes .d, 2 deps 2 dynamic edges in graph
dyndep/incremental_rebuild.toml / no_rebuild_unrelated.toml Modify declared / unrelated source Only dependent rebuilt / zero dispatched
dyndep/cycle.toml Dynamic edge creates A→B→A DynamicCycle
dyndep/suspend_resume.toml Node suspended, dep resolves Resumes and completes correctly
dyndep/persist_reload.toml Build, kill, restart Same edges loaded, no under-build
dyndep/monadic.toml Engine-level dep request (no depfile) Suspends mid-eval, resumes after dep
dyndep/large.toml 1000 actions × 3 dynamic deps each All edges registered, no deadlock

Malformed: NUL bytes in depfile → parser error logged, dynamic deps skipped, node continues. Replay: stable depfile deps across runs → no redundant re-registration.

8.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Recovery
Dynamic dep on already-Clean node Double-dispatch add_dynamic_dep after completion Check node state first; skip increment if already Clean
Depfile includes system headers Thousands of spurious edges No source-root filtering Filter paths outside project root (ibg.toml list)
Cycle-check DFS under write lock Deadlock if DFS enters a lock-holding thread's node Lock nesting Cycle check read-lock only; write-lock after check passes
Suspend without waker registered Node stuck forever Dep completes before SuspendSet insert Register waker BEFORE checking dep state (double-check pattern)
Persisted edge for deleted source NodeNotFound on load Source removed between builds Validate dep exists on load; drop stale edge + warn
Double registration / monadic request loop Stuck Pending / infinite suspend-resume cycle Concurrent parsers for same action / eval bug Dedup via HashMap::entry; error after 3 re-suspensions

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • DFS cycle check is O(V+E) worst case; an incremental topo-order-numbering check (topo_order[dep] < topo_order[from]) makes it O(1) amortized at 100K nodes.
  • DynamicEdgeRegistry's RwLock<HashMap> shards by from_node_id % 64 under high-concurrency writes; SuspendSet DashMap lookups are O(1) per dep completion (sharding suffices, avoid cloning values).
  • Depfile I/O is async (tokio::fs, overlaps CAS write); dynamic-edge DB writes batch into one transaction per build; suspend/resume semaphore churn is ~100 ns (tokio lock-free), acceptable; SmallVec<[NodeId;4]> avoids heap allocation for typical awaiting_deps.

8.8 Edge Cases

  • Dynamic dep is itself dynamic-dep-bearing: resolves recursively, depth bounded by DAG diameter; dep points to a Glob node: handled as a source node post-expansion.
  • Empty depfile: valid, zero edges registered; depfile dep is the action's own output: self-loop, caught by cycle detection.
  • Timeout while suspended: ActionFailed, SuspendSet cleaned up, notifier must not wake a failed node; build cancelled while suspended: SuspendSet drained, all suspended nodes Skipped, slots released.
  • Dynamic edge to an already-Clean node (incremental case): dep.last_changed <= node.last_verified skips re-suspend; two actions discover the same dep concurrently: only the first increments in-degree (Entry::or_insert dedup).

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery Operator Signal
Depfile parse error Err, logged Dynamic deps for that action missing (possible future under-build) Fix depfile format; rebuild WARN DyndepParseError{node_id,path}
Dynamic cycle detected CycleError from add_edge Single build fails, graph not mutated Fix action to avoid the cycle ERROR with edge (from,dep)
Persist write failure for dynamic edges Err on table write Edges lost, re-discovered next build via depfile Fix disk/permissions PersistError::DynamicEdgeWriteFailed
SuspendSet deadlock (waker bug) Node stuck, build hangs Watchdog fires at 60s Fix waker registration race ERROR with suspended node + awaited deps
Stale persisted edge after restructure Load-time validation Startup warning, edge dropped Foreign-key check recommended WARN StaleEdgeDropped{from,dep}

8.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: dynamic_dep_discovered_total, node_suspended_count, node_resume_latency_ms, depfile_parse_error_count, dynamic_cycle_detected_count; tracing spans dyndep/suspend/resume.
  • Config (ibg.toml): dyndep.depfile_filter_roots (default ["."]), dyndep.max_dynamic_deps_per_node (10000), dyndep.persist_dynamic_edges (true); ibg-cli graph --show-dynamic renders dynamic edges as dashed DOT edges with discovered_at labels.
  • Rollback: a dynamic-edge schema change bumps ibg-persist's version, forcing one full rebuild (edges re-discovered from depfiles) — no silent under-build possible.
  • DynamicEdgeRegistry is internal to ibg-graph; accessed via the DynDepRegistrar trait so third-party executors can implement their own discovery; debug with IBG_LOG=ibg_graph::dynamic=trace,ibg_engine::suspend=trace correlated against DynamicDepDiscovered events.

21. Phase 9 — File watching + invalidation — fs-event watcher, polling fallback, debounce, dirty propagation through reverse edges, watchman-style query, incremental re-eval of affected subgraph

9.1 Objectives

  • ibg-watch: fs-event watcher (inotify/kqueue/FSEvents) + polling fallback with identical semantics; tracks path→Vec<NodeId> and reverse edges (sourced from ibg-graph) for dirty propagation.
  • Debounce coalescing collapses event bursts into one invalidation signal per path within a configurable window (default 50 ms); exposes WatchHandle::changed_nodes()->Vec<NodeId> for targeted re-eval, no full-rebuild default.
  • Watchman-style query interface subscribes per glob/path set and returns the delta since a Revision (stateless fallback for CI).
  • Invariant: the dirty propagation set is always a superset of actually-changed transitive deps — never a subset (false-negative = correctness bug); false positives (over-build) are acceptable, under-build is not.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Modules: lib.rs (WatchHandle/WatchConfig/WatchEvent), backend/{mod,inotify,kqueue,poll}.rs, debounce.rs, propagate.rs, query.rs, metrics.rs.

WatchBackend trait: watch_paths(paths), unwatch_paths(paths), poll_events(timeout)->Result<Vec<RawEvent>,IbgError>; RawEvent{path,kind:Create|Modify|Delete|Rename{from,to}}.

Debounce: DebounceBatch=HashMap<PathBuf,(EventKind,Instant)>, flushed when the oldest entry exceeds debounce_window (ticked via crossbeam_channel::after); merges deduplicate last-write-wins; a rename is treated as delete(from)+create(to), both paths enqueued.

Path→NodeId index (propagate.rs): PathIndex=HashMap<PathBuf,SmallVec<[NodeId;2]>> built from ibg-graph source nodes at startup and updated incrementally on GraphEvent::NodeAdded/NodeRemoved; propagate_dirty(paths,reverse_index)->HashSet<NodeId> BFS's from source nodes through ibg-graph::reverse_deps (Static edges only — dynamic edges resolve during engine re-eval), producing DirtySet{nodes,revision_fence}.

WatchHandle API: new(config,graph)->Result<Self,IbgError>, start(), changed_since(rev)->DirtySet, subscribe(pattern)->Subscription, stop().

Polling fallback (ADR-09): StatCache=HashMap<PathBuf,(mtime,size,ino)> diffed each tick (default 500 ms interval); accuracy bound is one poll interval (documented max-staleness guarantee).

Watchman-style query: QueryRequest{since,expression:Expr}→QueryResult{changed,clock}; Expr::Suffix|Glob|AnyOf|AllOf filters the debounce log by since.

ibg-engine integration: daemon mode's run_watch_loop() blocks on the WatchHandle and calls eval_targets(dirty_set) per arrival; one-shot mode uses poll_once(timeout) as a CI pre-check.

Observability: WatchMetrics{events_received,events_debounced,dirty_nodes_emitted,propagation_latency_us}; span!(watch.propagate) around the dirty BFS.

Tests: tempfile::TempDir file writes asserting changed_since(rev).nodes matches the expected NodeId set; MockBackend injects RawEvents for deterministic unit tests without a real FS watch.

9.3 Deliverable

  • ibg-watch compiles; start() registers paths from ibg-graph source nodes; daemon loop (edit→debounce→propagation→incremental re-eval) completes <200 ms on a 10k-node graph.
  • ibg watch <targets> CLI holds the loop and prints invalidated nodes per build; polling fallback auto-activates on unsupported kernels (logged); examples/watch_demo prints changed nodes live.

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
Debounce coalescing + BFS propagation ≥10 rapid events → 1; depth-5 graph propagation correct, 100% pass
Integration: targeted re-eval No spurious nodes beyond the expected set
Latency (inotify / polling) ≤100 ms p99 / ≤poll_interval+10 ms
False-negative dirty propagation 0 (correctness invariant)
Rename + 100k-node bench Both from/to paths propagated; invalidation ≤500 ms

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
watch_single_source_edit.toml Edit one source dirty={NodeId(42)}
watch_burst_debounce.toml 50 rapid writes, same file Exactly 1 dirty event
watch_rename.toml rename src→src2 Dirty includes both source nodes
watch_delete_recreate.toml Delete+recreate within window Treated as modify
watch_deep_fanout.toml 1 source → 500 transitive actions All 500 in dirty set
watch_unregistered_path.toml Event outside PathIndex Silently ignored, no panic
watch_poll_fallback.toml Forced polling backend Dirty set ≤2 intervals latency
watch_cycle_guard.toml BFS on graph with removed dyn edge Terminates, no infinite loop

9.6 Pitfalls

  • Kernel overflow (inotify queue full): silent under-build if unhandled; detected via IN_Q_OVERFLOW/watch.kernel_overflow_count, recovered by a full re-stat of registered paths.
  • Cross-directory rename: split IN_MOVED_FROM/IN_MOVED_TO cookie pairs degrade to unmatched delete+create (acceptable over-build) — always re-register the new path so the create is never missed.
  • Symlink tracking: inotify watches the inode not the name, so editing through a symlink can miss the registered canonical path; mitigated by canonicalizing all paths at registration.
  • FSEvents coalescing (macOS): OS batches events over ~100 ms; the debounce window must exceed it or de-duplication has no effect.
  • Stale PathIndex after graph mutation: a source node added mid-watch is invisible until sync_graph() — the engine calls it after every graph-mutating build.
  • BFS on high-fan-in graphs: O(E) traversal can spike propagation latency; watched via the propagation_latency_us tail. Watch startup race: register paths before reading digests so a pre-watch edit isn't missed.

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • PathIndex interns paths (Arc<str>/ahash) rather than allocating a fresh PathBuf per event; BFS reuses a persistent u32-indexed bitset instead of a fresh HashSet<NodeId> per propagation.
  • Debounce map is capped at 64k entries (overflow flushes immediately); inotify fd count is bounded by the Linux default 8192-watch limit — mitigated with recursive directory watches.
  • crossbeam_channel::bounded(4096) provides backpressure between the backend and debounce threads; polling costs one stat syscall per path per tick (10k paths × 500 ms ≈ 20k stat/s, documented); PathIndex's RwLock upgrades to DashMap if read contention exceeds 5%.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty dirty set: all changed paths unregistered → engine skips re-eval; graph replaced mid-watch: stop, rebuild PathIndex, restart (no partial updates during teardown).
  • NFS/remote FS: ENODEV on watch registration auto-falls back to polling with an operator warning; rapid graph churn: BFS holds a read lock while dynamic-dep discovery mutates the reverse index — writer-preference RwLock bounds starvation.
  • Cyclic reverse-edge (defensive): impossible in a correct DAG, but the BFS visited-set still prevents an infinite loop and emits CycleDetected if triggered; path >4096 bytes: detected and errored at registration.
  • Watch handle dropped: stop token exits the backend thread with a synchronous debounce flush first; duplicate watch handles on one path: deduplicated via reference-counted inotify watch descriptors.

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Kernel overflow → missed events IN_Q_OVERFLOW metric spike Silent under-build for affected nodes Full re-stat registered paths; rebuild dirty set
PathIndex stale (untracked new node) Digest mismatch at verify Single node stale build sync_graph(); warn operator
Backend thread panic JoinHandle error, watchdog Watch loop stops Restart WatchHandle; log backtrace
Polling gap (double edit in one interval) Second edit invisible Stale output until next edit Documented max-staleness; force rebuild
BFS runaway (degenerate graph) propagation_latency_us > threshold Blocked invalidation delivery Timeout BFS at 1s; emit full dirty set

9.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: watch_events_total, watch_debounced_total, watch_dirty_nodes_total, watch_propagation_latency_us (p50/p95/p99), watch_kernel_overflow_total, watch_backend label; tracing watch.debounce/watch.propagate/watch.sync_graph spans.
  • Logging: INFO backend selection, WARN overflow/symlink/polling-fallback, DEBUG per event batch; ibg.toml [watch] config: debounce_ms, poll_interval_ms, max_watch_fds.
  • Watch is opt-in per invocation (disabling is stateless, one-shot mode resumes cleanly); PathIndex rebuilds from ibg-graph on every WatchHandle::new() (no persistent watch state); the polling backend runs anywhere, including containers with no inotify.

22. Phase 10 — Persistence — durable incremental state DB across invocations (graph, node states, revisions, digests), crash-consistent writes, schema-versioned, fall back to full eval on corruption

10.1 Objectives

  • ibg-persist: durable storage of {BuildGraph snapshot, NodeState per node, global Revision, per-path Digest cache} across invocations; crash-consistent (torn write → detect → full-rebuild fallback, never serve stale state from a corrupt DB).
  • SCHEMA_VERSION:u32 in the DB header; incompatible version → wipe + full rebuild, no manual migration below the version-gap threshold.
  • Performance goal: load 100k-node state <200 ms, flush incremental delta <100 ms per build; single-writer (engine), multi-reader OK via snapshot reads (telemetry/inspect).
  • Invariant: Revision monotonicity survives restarts (loaded counter never regresses below the last saved one). Out of scope: distributed multi-writer, ACID beyond single-process crash-consistency.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Modules: lib.rs (StateDb/DbConfig/DbSnapshot), storage/{mod,redb,sled}.rs (StorageBackend trait), schema.rs, snapshot.rs, gc.rs, migration.rs, metrics.rs.

Storage choice: redb (Rust-native, no unsafe LMDB FFI, MVCC read + single-writer, WAL + copy-on-write crash-consistency, no external process); sled is a feature-gated fallback.

Schema: table meta holds schema_version:u32 and revision:u64; node_states:NodeId(u32)→NodeStateRecord (bincode, one row/node); node_keys:NodeId→NodeKey; digest_cache:PathBuf→(Digest,mtime_ns); dynamic_deps:NodeId→Vec<DynamicDep>. NodeStateRecord{last_verified:u64,last_changed:u64,output_digest:Option<[u8;32]>,status_code:u8} is compact and fixed-size.

Open (StateDb::open): opens the redb file at state_dir/ibg.db; missing schema_version → fresh DB, writes current version; mismatched version → migration::handle always wipes and returns NeedsFullRebuild; reads meta["revision"] to seed the global counter; header checksum validation is handled by redb's internal WAL replay.

Flush (flush_delta): called by the engine after each build in one redb write transaction: write meta["revision"], upsert dirty NodeStateRecord rows (only touched nodes) and new digest_cache entries, delete rows for removed nodes; the transaction's WAL fsyncs before returning Ok.

Load (load_snapshot): a read-only transaction iterates node_states into a HashMap<NodeId,NodeState> in engine memory, returning DbSnapshot{revision,node_count,load_duration}.

Crash-consistency (P15): redb's WAL discards uncommitted transactions on replay (all-or-nothing visibility); meta["revision"] is written last in the transaction, so a header missing post-crash forces a wipe; StateDb::open surfaces DbCorrupt and the engine treats it as a warned cold start.

GC: StateDb::gc(live_nodes) deletes node_states rows outside the live set; digest_cache entries older than digest_cache_ttl (default 7 days) are evicted; runs at build end when gc_interval has elapsed.

Observability: persist_load_ms, persist_flush_ms, persist_node_count, persist_db_bytes, persist_schema_mismatch_total, persist_corrupt_total.

Tests: open/flush/reload unit cycle; crash simulation (partial transaction via redb raw page injection asserts the wipe path); schema-bump test (v1 DB opened by v2 code → NeedsFullRebuild).

10.3 Deliverable

  • StateDb::open/flush_delta/load_snapshot functional; end-to-end build→persist→kill→rebuild loads prior NodeState and re-evaluates only changed nodes.
  • ibg inspect-db <path> prints {schema_version,revision,node_count,db_bytes}; ibg-bench persist_roundtrip measures 100k-node flush+reload throughput.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
Load 100k records / flush 1k dirty nodes ≤200 ms / ≤100 ms
Flush 100k dirty (cold write) ≤2 s
Crash recovery + schema mismatch 100% wipe+rebuild triggered; ≥99.9% invocations correct
Revision monotonicity (1000 restarts) 0 regressions
DB size (100k nodes) + round-trip equality ≤50 MB; byte-identical flush→reload

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
persist_roundtrip_basic.toml 10-node graph, flush, reload All NodeStates equal
persist_crash_mid_write.bin WAL torn at byte 4096 DbCorrupt; engine full-rebuild
persist_schema_v0.db Old schema version file NeedsFullRebuild; wipe triggered
persist_revision_monotone.toml 100 restart cycles 0 non-monotonic violations
persist_gc_orphan.toml Flush 100, remove 30, gc db rows == 70
persist_large_graph.toml 100k-node flush+reload benchmark Within thresholds
persist_digest_cache_ttl.toml Insert, advance time, gc Stale entries evicted
persist_concurrent_inspect.toml Writer + reader simultaneously Reader sees consistent snapshot

10.6 Pitfalls

  • Write transaction held open too long: blocks GC/inspect readers; flush only at the build-end boundary, never during action execution.
  • Revision written before node states: crash between them leaves a stale revision on reload (safe over-rebuild, never under-rebuild) — validated by checking loaded revision against the max last_changed in node_states.
  • Bincode schema drift: adding a NodeStateRecord field without a SCHEMA_VERSION bump silently deserializes with a wrong default; CI asserts that decoding v_prev bytes with the v_current codec errors.
  • DB on tmpfs/ramdisk: fsync is a no-op there, so a crash loses the last transaction — safe for rebuild but persistence shouldn't be relied on; warn if a tmpfs mount is detected.
  • Unbounded digest_cache growth: a 1M-file repo at 64 bytes/entry is 64 MB — capped by TTL GC + a size cap (default 200 MB).
  • Concurrent open from two processes: redb's file lock makes the second open return DbLocked ("another build in progress"), never silently succeeding.
  • NodeId reuse after graph wipe: mitigated by wiping the DB whenever a graph_fingerprint mismatch is detected in meta.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • Row deserialization at 100k rows is CPU-bound; rayon parallelizes chunked table-scan decode; redb's default 32 MB page cache is tuned via set_cache_size(min(ram/4,512MB)).
  • CoW page writes are batched into one transaction to minimize B-tree splits; NodeStateRecord is ~40 bytes (4 MB raw / ~1 MB zstd-compressed at 100k rows) if DB I/O becomes the bottleneck.
  • digest_cache's in-memory map uses FxHashMap over default SipHash for u32-key throughput; GC is a full O(rows) scan, run async post-build and rate-limited to one per gc_interval_secs; spinning-disk startup I/O may miss the 200 ms load target — SSD is the documented requirement (memory-only fallback for CI).

10.8 Edge Cases

  • DB file deleted externally mid-build: flush detects the missing file, returns DbCorrupt, skips persist for that build; disk full during flush: WAL write fails, transaction rolls back, prior state intact, DiskFull surfaced.
  • NodeId u32 overflow: >4B nodes unsupported, detected and panics clearly at allocation; concurrent read+write: redb MVCC gives readers the last committed snapshot with no blocking.
  • Clock skew (mtime_ns regression): mitigated by always also comparing size, treating a clock-skewed entry as dirty; zero-byte DB file: interpreted as fresh, acceptable.
  • Read-only FS: StateDb::open returns ReadOnlyFs, engine runs ephemeral (no persist) with a warning; symlinked state_dir: canonicalized at open, warned, canonical path used for the lock file.

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Corrupt DB (WAL torn) / schema mismatch Replay error or version mismatch Full rebuild triggered Wipe DB; cold start
Disk full during flush Err on WAL write State not saved, next build cold Free disk; retry
Two processes open DB DbLocked Second build aborts Wait for first to finish
graph_fingerprint mismatch meta[graph_fp] != current Stale NodeIds resurface Wipe; rebuild
Revision regression (bug) Loaded rev < persisted rev Possible over-build Assert + force wipe

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: persist_load_duration_ms, persist_flush_duration_ms, persist_node_count, persist_db_bytes, persist_schema_mismatch_total, persist_corrupt_wipe_total, persist_gc_duration_ms/_rows_deleted; tracing spans persist.load/.flush/.gc.
  • Logging: INFO on open (schema/revision/count), WARN on schema mismatch+wipe, ERROR on corrupt+fallback; ibg inspect-db --path dumps meta + sample states; ibg export-state --output <tar> for portable debugging archives.
  • Config: ibg.toml [persist] state_dir (default .ibg/state, overridable via IBG_STATE_DIR); downgrading the binary triggers an expected schema-mismatch wipe; same SCHEMA_VERSION is forward/backward compatible within patch versions, minor bumps may increment schema, major always wipes.

23. Phase 11 — Hermeticity + sandboxing — sandboxed action execution, undeclared-input detection (the #1 cache-correctness bug), declared-output enforcement, reproducibility verification

11.1 Objectives

  • ibg-sandbox: wrap every exec in a hermetic environment — controlled filesystem view, declared-input-only reads, declared-output-only writes, controlled env, controlled working dir.
  • Undeclared-input detection: any filesystem read outside Action::inputs fails loudly with UndeclaredInput{action,path}; declared-output enforcement fails any write outside Action::outputs with UndeclaredOutput.
  • Reproducibility verification: opt-in double-execution comparing output digests, mismatch → NonDeterministic{action,digest_a,digest_b}, logged and never cached.
  • Strategy: Linux (namespace+seccomp+LD_PRELOAD), macOS (sandbox-exec/LD_PRELOAD), CI-safe noop (output-diff only); target ≤50 ms setup for typical actions.
  • Cache-soundness invariant: a hit is correct ONLY if ActionKey covers every actual filesystem read — sandbox is the enforcement layer, and an undeclared-input error must halt the action (caching such a result is a correctness violation).

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Modules: lib.rs (SandboxConfig/SandboxedAction/SandboxResult), strategy/{mod,namespace,preload,sandbox_exec,noop}.rs, interceptor/{mod,shim.c,protocol}.rs, output_check.rs, repro.rs, allowlist.rs, metrics.rs.

SandboxStrategy trait: prepare(action,workdir)->Result<SandboxEnv,IbgError>, execute(env)->Result<RawExecResult,IbgError>, audit(env,result)->AuditReport.

Linux namespace strategy (ADR-10, following P6's linux-sandbox model): clone(CLONE_NEWUSER|CLONE_NEWNS) (rootless); bind-mounts declared inputs read-only into /sandbox/inputs/, the output dir read-write into /sandbox/outputs/, plus /proc//dev/null//dev/urandom; a seccomp filter allows declared syscalls and denies socket/ptrace/mount; the supervisor listens on a Unix socket outside the namespace, the shim connects via a pre-opened fd passed across clone.

LD_PRELOAD shim: intercepts open/openat/openat2/stat/lstat/access/fopen/creat, sending {call_type,path,flags} to the supervisor and receiving Allow|Deny|Redirect; the supervisor checks reads against the declared_inputs prefix-trie and writes against declared_outputs. Shim overhead is one IPC round-trip per syscall (~5–20 µs) — mitigated by a pre-seeded shared-memory allowlist segment so already-known paths never hit IPC.

Allowlist: PathTrie — compressed prefix trie over OS-path bytes, contains_or_prefix(path)->bool, seeded from canonicalized Action::inputs (O(depth) lookup), plus the sandbox workdir//proc/self//declared tool paths.

Output enforcement: post-exec walk of the sandbox output dir diffs written paths against declared_outputs (extras are undeclared-output errors, declared-but-missing paths are MissingOutput).

Reproducibility: SandboxConfig::repro_mode:ReproMode{Never|Probabilistic(f64)|Always}; on Always or a sampled hit, re-executes in a fresh sandbox and compares run-A/run-B digests, emitting NonDeterministic and skipping the cache on mismatch; a determinism registry (ActionKey→repro_pass:bool) in ibg-persist skips re-verification for known-deterministic actions.

macOS (ADR-11): generates a Seatbelt profile (allow declared-input reads, deny others, allow declared-output writes) invoked via sandbox-exec -f; undeclared reads surface as EPERM, intercepted as UndeclaredInput.

CI-safe/noop: no isolation, direct run, output-diff only (extra writes undetectable) — documented as not hermetic, for trusted actions or kernel-feature-less CI only.

ibg-exec integration: run_sandboxed(action,strategy)->ExecResult carrying AuditReport{undeclared_inputs,undeclared_outputs,repro_status}; any undeclared-input marks the action Failed and skips the cache store, with a telemetry event.

Observability: SandboxMetrics{setup_ms,undeclared_input_total,undeclared_output_total,repro_mismatch_total,strategy_label}; spans sandbox.prepare/sandbox.audit.

Tests: undeclared read of /etc/hostname → UndeclaredInput; write to an undeclared path → UndeclaredOutput; a timestamp-embedding action → NonDeterministic; a stable action → repro pass, cached next time.

11.3 Deliverable

  • Linux namespace + LD_PRELOAD strategy fully functional; macOS sandbox-exec CI-tested; ibg build --sandbox=namespace|preload|exec|noop selects strategy.
  • ibg-bench sandbox_overhead measures per-action setup latency vs. unsandboxed; an undeclared-input action fails the build with a clear violating-path error; --repro=always executes each action twice and reports mismatches.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Threshold
Undeclared read detected (namespace / LD_PRELOAD) 100% / ≥95% (shim may miss raw syscall(2))
Undeclared / missing output detected 100% / 100%
Sandbox setup latency (namespace / preload) ≤50 ms p99 / ≤10 ms p99
No false-positive on declared inputs 100%
Repro non-determinism detection ≥99% (statistical, probabilistic mode)
Cache: undeclared-input action never cached 100%
Allowlist trie lookup <1 µs/query

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
sandbox_undeclared_read_etc.toml / declared_read_ok.toml Reads /etc/hostname / only declared UndeclaredInput / succeeds
sandbox_undeclared_write.toml / missing_output.toml Writes /tmp/foo / skips declared output UndeclaredOutput / MissingOutput
sandbox_repro_stable.toml / repro_nondeterministic.toml Stable action / embeds date +%s Digests match, cached / NonDeterministic, not cached
sandbox_network_denied.toml Action calls curl seccomp EPERM or sandbox deny
sandbox_tool_allowed.toml Compiler in declared tools Succeeds
sandbox_noop_output_diff.toml noop mode, missing output MissingOutput

11.6 Pitfalls

  • LD_PRELOAD bypass via raw syscall(2): the shim cannot intercept it; documented limitation, mitigated by seccomp in the namespace strategy.
  • Canonicalization mismatch (symlink vs. realpath): false-positive UndeclaredInput; both declared and intercepted paths are canonicalized before trie lookup.
  • Shim IPC amplification: 10k stat calls at 5–20 µs round-trip cost 50–200 ms; mitigated by a shared-memory allowlist so only unknown paths hit IPC.
  • Rootless namespace setup failure: clone() returns EPERM when user namespaces are disabled; auto-falls back to LD_PRELOAD with a WARN.
  • macOS SIP restricts sandbox-exec: documented; LD_PRELOAD is the primary macOS strategy, sandbox-exec best-effort.
  • Repro mode doubles build time: mitigated by Probabilistic(0.05) default sampling and a persisted determinism registry that skips re-verification.
  • Glob inputs not fully enumerated: files added after graph construction trigger a legitimate undeclared-input error — mitigated by re-expanding glob nodes on watch invalidation.
  • Temporary files outside the declared output dir: mitigated by a private tmpfs mount at /tmp inside the namespace, so output-diff only checks the declared dir.

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Namespace clone+mount setup dominates short-action cost (<10 ms exec); amortized by pre-spawning a pool of pre-cloned namespace workers.
  • Shim IPC sockets are reused per-action, never recreated per intercepted call; allowlist trie memory is ~160 KB/action (1000 inputs × 10 path components), ~5 MB at 32 parallel actions — acceptable.
  • Repro double-exec doubles CAS writes (mitigated by comparing digests before the final store); the seccomp BPF filter compiles once at process start and is inherited by every clone; the post-exec output-dir walk parallelizes with rayon past 50 ms for 10k-file actions.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • Nonzero exit + undeclared input: both recorded, AuditReport preserves both, never hidden by exit code; empty declared inputs: any read at all is UndeclaredInput (legitimate for pure-compute actions).
  • Declared output that is a directory: walked recursively, all files count as declared; symlink writes to a non-declared target: output-check follows the link and checks the target path too.
  • Action spawns child processes: children inherit namespace/seccomp/shim env, their reads are intercepted too (verified by a multi-process test); SIGKILL during clone(): kernel cleans up the orphan namespace on parent exit.
  • Repro digest comparison on inherently non-deterministic output (e.g. embedded timestamps): users exclude such files via repro_exclude_patterns; action modifies its (read-only) input mount: EROFS, action fails, expected and documented.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Undeclared input slips through (shim bypass) Wrong cache hit served silently Incorrect build Add namespace/seccomp; audit with strace
Namespace clone fails (kernel restriction) EPERM on clone() Strategy disabled Auto-fallback to preload; WARN
IPC deadlock (shim blocked on supervisor) sandbox_setup_ms spike, action hangs Single action blocked Timeout IPC (5s); kill action; report
Repro mismatch cache poisoning (bug) Both runs correct but differ Wrong hit if ignored Never cache on mismatch (enforced in code)
macOS SIP blocks sandbox-exec Operation not permitted macOS sandbox disabled Fall back to preload; warn
Private /tmp fills disk Large temp writes Disk full, action fails tmpfs size limit (512m); cleaned on exit

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: sandbox_prepare_ms (p50/p95/p99), sandbox_undeclared_input/output_total, sandbox_missing_output_total, sandbox_repro_mismatch/pass_total, sandbox_strategy label; spans sandbox.prepare/.audit/.repro.
  • Logging: DEBUG per allow/deny (gated), WARN on undeclared input, ERROR on repro mismatch, INFO on strategy selection; optional JSONL audit log (ibg.toml [sandbox] audit_log) records {ts,action_id,path,access_type,verdict}.
  • Strategy auto-detect ladder: namespace → preload → exec → noop (ibg.toml [sandbox] strategy); Docker --privileged enables namespace, standard Docker/GH Actions use preload (macOS: exec).
  • Changing strategy mid-project needs no cache invalidation (ActionKey is strategy-independent); the repro determinism registry is schema-versioned with the main persist DB and wiped together on mismatch.
  • Security model: ibg-sandbox is a correctness tool (undeclared-input detection), not a security boundary against adversarial actions — documented distinction.

24. Phase 12 — Remote cache / remote execution interface — content-addressed remote cache protocol, Merkle-tree input trees, upload/download of action results, REAPI-style abstraction (pluggable)

12.1 Objectives

  • ibg-remote: REAPI-style pluggable RemoteBackend trait covering CAS blobs + ActionCache + optional RemoteExecution; Merkle-tree input construction from declared inputs (directory digest = hash of sorted dir-proto).
  • Lazy upload (missing blobs only) + download of action results into local CAS, integrated with ibg-cache/ibg-exec; pluggable backends NullRemote (default), GrpcRemote (tonic/prost, REAPI v2), HttpRemote (bazel-remote HTTP/1.1).
  • Correctness: every remote hit is digest-verified; mismatch → reject + fall through to local exec, never a silent wrong result.
  • Scope: read-through (remote check before local exec), write-through (upload after exec), optional remote execution; opt-in remote feature flag — local-only builds compile without tonic.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Trait (lib.rs): async fn find_missing(digests)->Result<Vec<Digest>>, upload_blob/batch_upload/download_blob, get_action_result(key)->Result<Option<RemoteActionResult>>, update_action_result, optional execute(req)->Result<RemoteExecStream>, capabilities()->RemoteCapabilities.

Merkle input tree (merkle.rs): InputTree::build(inputs:&[(PathBuf,Digest)])->MerkleDirectory sorts entries and builds a nested MerkleDirectory{digest,subdirs:BTreeMap<OsStr,MerkleDirectory>,files:BTreeMap<OsStr,FileNode>} (purely structural, no I/O); collect_blobs(tree) flattens and dedupes by Digest for batch upload; paths are normalized relative to the workspace root, .. traversal rejected.

Upload (upload.rs): Uploader::upload_inputs(backend,inputs) collects (path,Digest) pairs from the fingerprint cache, builds the MerkleDirectory, calls find_missing to filter already-present blobs, then batch-uploads the rest in ≤4 MB chunks, returning the root tree for ActionKey embedding; transient network errors retry with exponential backoff (3 attempts, 200 ms base), classified Retriable/Fatal.

Download (download.rs): Downloader::materialize(backend,result,out_dir) checks local CAS first, download_blobs on miss, verifies blake3(bytes)==digest (reject + metric on mismatch), writes to local CAS, then materializes via ibg-exec symlink/copy; blobs >8 MB stream rather than buffer fully.

GrpcRemote (feature remote-grpc, ADR-12/ADR-13): {cas_client,ac_client,exec_client:Option<_>,instance_name} maps trait methods to REAPI calls (BatchUpdateBlobs, BatchReadBlobs, GetActionResult, UpdateActionResult, Execute) through an interceptor chain (auth token, deadline propagation, retry budget); RemoteCapabilities::from_proto queries GetCapabilities at startup.

HttpRemote (feature remote-http): bazel-remote HTTP/1.1 (GET/PUT /cas/{hash}/{size}, GET/PUT /ac/{hash}) via reqwest with connection pooling; no find_missing, so every blob attempts a GET and treats 404 as missing.

Integration: on a local ibg-cache miss the engine calls remote.get_action_result(key), materializing + inserting into local CAS/AC on a hit; after a successful local exec it fires update_action_result+batch_upload as a background tokio::spawn task (failure logged, non-fatal). RemoteConfig{backend,endpoint,instance_name,tls,max_batch_bytes,concurrency} loads from the ibg-cli config.

Observability: counters remote_cache_hit/miss, remote_upload/download_bytes, remote_error; histograms remote_upload/download/find_missing_latency_ms; spans ibg.remote.upload/.download/.get_action_result tagged action_key/digest/backend_kind.

Tests: MockRemoteBackend (in-memory blob+AC maps implementing the full trait); tests/remote_roundtrip.rs (upload→miss→exec→update→second build hits remote, byte-identical outputs); tests/merkle_tree.rs (deterministic root digest, complete deduplicated blob list).

12.3 Deliverable

  • ibg-remote compiles both --no-default-features (NullRemote only) and with --features remote-grpc,remote-http; ibg-cli build --remote-cache=grpc://host:port routes lookups through GrpcRemote, while the no-flag path behaves identically to Phase 11 (remote is fully transparent).
  • MockRemoteBackend integration test: a repeat identical build hits remote 100% (zero exec), outputs byte-identical; Merkle trees are deterministic across invocations and machines.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
cargo test -p ibg-remote + remote roundtrip (mock) 100% pass; 100% hit rate on repeat build
Digest mismatch injection Rejected, falls back to local exec, remote_error+1
Batch chunk compliance + find_missing dedup No batch >max_batch_bytes; no duplicate uploads for repeated inputs
Merkle tree determinism Same input set → same root digest, 1000 iterations
GrpcRemote compile gate cargo check --features remote-grpc clean, no tonic without the feature
Upload / download latency <5 ms p99 (mock, 100 blobs) / <2 s (100 MB streaming, loopback)
Auth interceptor Token injected on every outbound gRPC call

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
remote/empty_inputs.json / nested_dirs.json Zero-input tree / 5-level nested tree blake3("") directory / root+subdir digests match reference
remote/duplicate_blobs.json Two inputs, same content, different paths Single blob upload, two FileNode entries
remote/digest_mismatch_response Server returns wrong bytes RemoteDigestMismatch, remote_error+1
remote/partial_batch_failure.json 3/5 blobs rejected by server Retries only failed blobs
remote/ac_not_found.json ActionCache returns 404 Falls through to local exec, no panic
remote/reapi_capabilities.json Server reports max_batch_size=1MB Uploader respects cap, splits accordingly
remote/stale_ac_entry AC hit but blob missing from CAS Download fails → fallback exec, remote_error counter

12.6 Pitfalls

  • Undeclared-input leakage to remote cache: sandbox-off actions cache a wrong result keyed only on declared inputs, poisoning every user of that cache — mitigated by always running sandbox in CI and tagging uploads sandbox=off.
  • Non-deterministic action poisons remote cache: mitigated by the reproducibility check (Phase 10/ibg-sandbox) and an optional --verify-reproducibility upload flag.
  • Merkle digest instability: non-canonical directory-proto serialization (field order/encoding) produces different root digests across language/version — fixed by lexicographic sorting and a fixed wire encoding.
  • find_missing N+1: per-blob calls instead of one batched call cause O(N) RPCs — always batch all digests per chunk.
  • Silent background-upload failure: a failed post-exec tokio::spawn upload leaves the local build succeeding with an unpopulated remote cache and no visible error — mitigated by a remote_upload_failure metric plus an optional --strict-upload flag.
  • Missing gRPC deadline propagation: an unforwarded build deadline lets remote exec outlive the build context — fixed by propagating CancellationToken through every RemoteBackend method.
  • TLS cert rotation mid-build: handshake failures mid-build are recovered by reconnect-with-retry-budget, surfaced as remote_error with a fallback to local exec.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Batch chunk size (default 4 MB) balances RPC count vs per-RPC memory; concurrency config gates simultaneous RPCs against server rate-limiting; blobs >8 MB stream, smaller ones buffer.
  • find_missing digests are deduplicated before the RPC (O(N log N) sort, acceptable); local CAS is always checked before upload to skip content already present remotely.
  • A single gRPC channel per backend is reused (tonic manages HTTP/2 multiplexing, no per-request reconnect); Merkle construction is O(N log N) and uses Arc<Bytes> to avoid cloning past 100k inputs; per-blob timeouts prevent one slow download from blocking the whole materialize.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty declared outputs: valid cache hit with an empty AC list, no download needed; Unicode/special-char paths: normalized to UTF-8 for digest, non-UTF-8 rejected as InvalidPath.
  • Remote unreachable at build start: get_action_result times out, engine falls back to local exec immediately (no hang), remote_unavailable metric increments.
  • Concurrent AC writes for the same key: idempotent for deterministic actions; non-deterministic actions are last-write-wins (acceptable, AC is a performance cache not ground truth).
  • Partial batch upload: retries only the rejected blobs, tracked by the returned digest list; clock skew: irrelevant, REAPI identity is digest-based not timestamp-based.
  • Directory tree >1M files: max_declared_inputs (default 500k) guards against Merkle-construction OOM; RemoteExecution timeout: falls back locally, records remote_exec_timeout, never caches a partial result.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Remote AC returns wrong digest blake3(bytes)!=expected on download Single action Reject; fallback local exec; file bug
Batch upload partial failure Non-OK per-blob status Affected build only Retry with backoff; fail build if persistent
Remote unavailable Connection timeout / gRPC UNAVAILABLE All remote-using builds Fall back to local exec; restore remote later
Non-deterministic action cached remotely Repro check mismatch on re-exec All future users of that key Evict AC entry (ibg-cache evict); fix action
TLS cert expired Handshake error on connect All remote-enabled builds Reconnect loop → fallback local; rotate cert
Merkle root collision (theoretical) Two input sets, same digest Cache soundness failure None at blake3 security level; upgrade hash if ever broken

12.10 Operational Notes

  • ibg-cli validates RemoteConfig (URL, TLS cert) at startup, failing fast with RemoteConfigInvalid; every metric is tagged backend=grpc|http|null; ibg-cli remote status queries GetCapabilities for deployment verification.
  • Adding remote-grpc to an existing binary is backward-compatible (no schema migration, NullRemote stays default); every AC write is audit-logged (action_key,executor_id,build_id,timestamp, 30-day retention).
  • --remote-cache-fallback=local (default on) makes remote failure non-fatal; --no-remote-fallback makes it fatal for CI-strict mode; traceparent propagation correlates remote server spans with the local build trace.

25. Phase 13 — Observability, conformance, differential, fuzzing, benchmarks — build metrics + profile (critical path, cache-hit rate), differential vs from-scratch full rebuild for correctness, fuzz graph mutations, large-graph + incremental-edit + differential harness

13.1 Objectives

  • ibg-telemetry: structured metrics (counters/histograms/gauges), OpenTelemetry tracing, build profile (critical-path attribution + per-node timing), Prometheus/OTLP export.
  • ibg-bench: large-graph benchmark harness, incremental-edit micro-benchmarks, differential correctness harness (incremental vs. from-scratch), cache-hit-rate measurement.
  • Differential correctness oracle: for any request+edit sequence, incremental_result == from_scratch_result (digest-equal) — detects under-build bugs.
  • Fuzzing (cargo-fuzz): graph mutation/cycle injection, fingerprint corruption, cache-key collisions, scheduler starvation; conformance suite covers every invariant (no under/over-build, early cutoff, cache soundness, hermeticity, cycle freedom, crash-consistency).
  • ibg-bench is a separate crate; benchmarks run under cargo bench (criterion), fuzz under cargo fuzz; no production-code changes for bench/fuzz.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

ibg-telemetry: Metrics{Arc<MetricsInner>} wraps opentelemetry_sdk::metrics::SdkMeterProvider as a process singleton, exposing counters (build_nodes_total{status}, cache_hits/misses_total, remote_cache_hits_total, actions_executed_total, early_cutoff_total, graph_cycles_detected_total), histograms (node_eval_duration_ms{kind}, action_exec_duration_ms, fingerprint_duration_us, cache_lookup_duration_us, scheduler_queue_wait_ms), and gauges (active_actions, scheduler_ready_queue_depth, graph_node/edge_count). Tracer wraps opentelemetry::global::tracer("ibg") with span constructors for eval/exec/cache-lookup/fingerprint; export is Prometheus pull (/metrics) and optional OTLP push (feature otlp).

BuildProfile{critical_path:Vec<NodeId>, critical_path_duration_ms, per_node:HashMap<NodeId,NodeTiming{queued_at,started_at,finished_at,cache_hit,exec_duration_ms:Option<u64>}>}; CriticalPathFinder::compute(graph,timings)->Vec<NodeId> is a reverse-topo Bellman-Ford over latency weights, O(V+E), run post-build. Every crate calls the process-global ibg_telemetry::metrics(); spans are zero-cost no-ops when the telemetry feature is off (ADR-15).

Differential harness (ibg-bench/src/differential.rs): DifferentialHarness{engine:IncrementalEngine, scratch_engine:ScratchEngine} where ScratchEngine is a fresh, unpersisted instance forced to full re-evaluation; run_differential(request,edits) applies edits, runs both engines, and compares incremental_outputs vs scratch_outputs — any mismatch is a correctness bug, returned as DifferentialResult{matches,divergences:Vec<(NodeId,Digest,Digest)>}. Edit{ModifyFile|AddFile|DeleteFile|AddNode|RemoveNode}; proptest generates random edit sequences asserting matches==true, shrinking on failure.

Fuzz targets (ibg-bench/fuzz/): fuzz_graph_mutation (arbitrary mutations, asserts CycleDetected iff a cycle was introduced), fuzz_fingerprint (verify_digest never panics), fuzz_cache_key (ActionKey::compute deterministic, never panics), fuzz_engine_eval (arbitrary BuildRequest on a random valid graph, no panic/deadlock, 5s timeout/iteration), fuzz_persist_load (arbitrary bytes as a DB snapshot, graceful error or valid state, never panics); each is #![no_main] + libfuzzer_sys::fuzz_target! with arbitrary::Arbitrary-derived inputs.

Benchmarks (ibg-bench/src/benches/): bench_large_graph.rs covers linear_chain_N (1k/10k/100k, cold+incremental timing), diamond_fan_N (parallel scheduling efficiency), deep_tree_D (critical-path + topo-sort cost), random_dag_N_E (construction + cycle check); bench_incremental_edit.rs measures single-file and 100-file batch edits in a 10k-node graph plus leaf-vs-root early-cutoff effectiveness; bench_cache.rs measures hit-rate throughput at 0/50/100% and 1M-digest CAS lookup latency. Criterion: 10s measurement, 3 warm-up iterations.

Conformance suite (ibg-bench/tests/conformance/, one module per invariant):

Module Invariant Test
no_under_build.rs / no_over_build.rs Changed input rebuilds / unchanged skips Edit file → node re-executed / unrelated edit → others untouched
early_cutoff.rs Unchanged output halts propagation Same-output injection → dependents not re-exec'd
cache_soundness.rs AC hit yields correct output Poisoned content, same key → not silently served
hermeticity.rs Undeclared read → error ibg-sandbox detects, never silently accepted
cycle_freedom.rs Cyclic dep addition errors Dynamic cycle attempt → CycleDetected, no deadlock
crash_consistency.rs / revision_monotonicity.rs Torn write → full re-eval / revisions never decrease Simulated SIGKILL correct next build / random sequence asserts monotonic
dynamic_dep.rs Runtime-discovered dep respected next build Discover then change dep → triggers rebuild

Observability integration: ibg-engine's eval_node span + early_cutoff_total; ibg-cache's cache_lookup_duration_us + hit/miss counters; ibg-sched's scheduler_queue_wait_ms + active_actions; ibg-fingerprint's fingerprint_duration_us + stat-fast-path hits; ibg-persist's write duration + load-error counters; ibg-remote's Phase-12 hooks — all aggregated into the same Metrics singleton.

13.3 Deliverable

  • ibg-telemetry serves all instruments on /metrics (OTLP export when enabled); cargo bench -p ibg-bench runs all criterion benches to target/criterion/; cargo fuzz run fuzz_graph_mutation (5 min) shows zero panics.
  • Differential (cargo test -- differential) passes 10k proptest iterations; conformance (-- conformance) 100% pass; ibg-cli build --profile emits JSON + human-readable critical-path/top-5-slowest summary.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
cargo test -p ibg-telemetry + conformance (9 modules) 100% pass
Differential (10k proptest) + fuzz (30 min each, nightly) 0 divergences; 0 panics/hangs
linear_chain_10k cold / incremental <2s (8 cores) / <100 ms
diamond_fan_1k scheduling efficiency >85% CPU utilization vs. serial
Single-file edit propagation (10k graph) <50 ms to dirty-set computed
Critical-path computation (100k nodes) + Prometheus scrape <200 ms; <5 ms p99
Cache hit-rate measurement accuracy ±1% vs. manual count

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
conf/no_under_build_base.graph + _edit.patch Baseline graph, then a rebuild-triggering edit Affected nodes re-exec'd, digests updated
conf/early_cutoff.graph Content-stable action output Dependents' last_verified updated, no re-exec
conf/cache_soundness_poison.bin AC entry with mismatched digest CacheCorruption; fallback exec
conf/cycle_dynamic.ops Ops introducing a dynamic-dep cycle CycleDetected before deadlock
conf/crash_mid_persist.bin Truncated persist DB PersistCorrupt; full re-eval
conf/revision_monotonic.ops 1000 random eval steps All sequences non-decreasing
bench/large_graph_100k.graph / incremental_edit_set.json 100k-node DAG / 100 sequential edits Bench baseline (git-lfs) / per-edit timing trace
fuzz/corpus/graph_mutation/ Seed corpus 50 hand-crafted boundary-case seeds

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Differential state bleed: sharing CAS dir/persist DB between the scratch and incremental engine yields false passes — the scratch engine must use a fully isolated tempdir().
  • Slow proptest shrinking on large graphs: capped at max_shrink_iters=500 with the minimal reproducer logged manually.
  • Criterion instability on CI: mitigated with --measurement-time 30 and a pinned CRITERION_HOME; flagged flaky above 10% coefficient of variation.
  • Fuzz deadlock masked by libfuzzer's timeout: reports "slow unit" not "hang" — fuzz_engine_eval needs an explicit tokio::time::timeout inside the target.
  • Metric cardinality explosion: never label by NodeId (unbounded) — aggregate by NodeKind/status only.
  • Critical path wrong on cache hits: a None exec_duration_ms must count as 0, never skip the node, or the critical path silently excludes cache-hit nodes.
  • Conformance flakiness from wall-clock ordering: use the logical Revision counter for assertions, not Instant.
  • Over-broad no-over-build assertions: scope to non-glob, non-dynamic-dep nodes since glob expansion can legitimately differ.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • Counter increments are a lock-free AtomicU64::fetch_add; histograms use pre-declared fixed bucket boundaries to avoid a per-recording lock.
  • Span creation at 100k nodes/build is sampled (1:100 production, 1:1 under --profile, via OTEL_TRACES_SAMPLER_ARG); differential from-scratch I/O dominates large-graph runs, so bench environments use a tmpfs tempdir.
  • Criterion sample storage and fuzz corpora are capped (CRITERION_HOME=/tmp, corpus capped at 10k with weekly cargo fuzz cmin); critical-path Bellman-Ford is O(V·E) worst case but acceptable for sparse build graphs (falls back to a single topo-sort pass if ever dense); Prometheus scrape and OTLP batch export never block on metric computation.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • Zero-node build request: both differential engines return an equal empty result, no panic; all-cache-hit build: critical path is validly 0, not a missing path.
  • Fuzz seed graph with a cycle: fuzz_engine_eval must return CycleDetected within timeout, never deadlock; no-op edit (rewritten same content): zero dirty nodes, differential trivially passes.
  • Edit sequence with an invalid intermediate state (delete then re-add): only the final state is compared; build_nodes_total u64 overflow: ~1.8e19, non-issue in practice.
  • Benchmark panic during criterion warm-up: caught per-sample, marked failed, suite continues; telemetry compiled out: all metric/span calls are zero-overhead stub no-ops.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Differential divergence (under-build bug) incremental_outputs != scratch_outputs Correctness of affected nodes Block release; fix eval logic; re-run
Fuzz crash discovered libfuzzer crash-* artifact Potential prod panic Reproduce, fix root cause, add regression fixture
Metric cardinality OOM Prometheus OOM/scrape timeout Monitoring blind Remove high-cardinality labels; restart
Conformance regression (CI) Module test failure Invariant broken in last PR Bisect; block merge; revert
Critical-path infinite loop Build hangs post-exec (CI timeout) Single build Timeout+kill; add cycle guard
OTLP export failure exporter_errors_total Trace data loss (non-fatal) Check endpoint health; reconnect

13.10 Operational Notes

  • ibg-cli build --profile emits build_profile.json (critical_path,per_node_timings,cache_hit_rate,total_actions,early_cutoffs); Prometheus scrapes http://localhost:9399/metrics (IBG_METRICS_PORT), all metrics prefixed ibg_; Grafana dashboard at docs/dashboards/ibg-build.json.
  • Nightly CI fuzzes each target 30 min with corpus synced to S3; any PR touching ibg-engine/ibg-fingerprint/ibg-graph must pass the 10k-iteration differential suite before merge.
  • profile_schema_version is additive-backward-compatible (breaking bumps to a new major with a CHANGELOG migration note); cargo build --features telemetry,otlp for full observability, --no-default-features for a minimal binary.
  • Benchmark baselines live on a bench-baselines/ branch (regression thresholds: 15% latency, 10% throughput); production trace sampling defaults to TraceIdRatioBased(0.01), --profile forces AlwaysOn.

Threat Model

Trust Boundaries

Boundary Trusted? Notes
Build-graph API caller (same process) Yes Co-located driver; no auth layer
Action command strings (from BUILD files) No User-supplied; validate before exec
File system (workspace) Partial Source files trusted; sandbox enforces declared I/O
Remote cache backend No Auth required; digest verification mandatory on fetch
Remote execution backend No TLS + HMAC on result; Digest-check output Merkle tree
ibg-persist DB file No CRC / BLAKE3 integrity checks; never trust blindly after crash
Watch events (OS kernel) Partial Race window between event and re-stat; always re-hash

Attack Surface Inventory

Vector Class Mitigation Detection Recovery
Malformed NodeKey / ActionKey from API Input validation Reject non-UTF-8, oversized keys; enforce schema in ibg-core IbgError::InvalidKey returned No-op; caller retries with valid input
Cycle injection via dynamic dep Graph integrity Cycle-check on every edge insert; DFS O(V+E) IbgError::CycleDetected Reject dep; propagate error to requesting node
Replay attack on action cache Cache correctness ActionKey = BLAKE3(cmd || sorted inputs || env || tool_digest); time not included → replay correct Log cache-hit source + ActionKey Poison check: re-hash a sample of cached outputs
Hash collision (BLAKE3 second-preimage) Cryptographic BLAKE3 256-bit; probability negligible; defence-in-depth: store content length alongside Digest Alert on Digest size mismatch for known-length objects Evict colliding entry; rebuild
Undeclared input exfiltration (sandbox bypass) Hermeticity / correctness ibg-sandbox intercepts fs calls (seccomp/BPF on Linux, sandbox-exec on macOS); deny undeclared reads SandboxViolation event → IbgError::UndeclaredInput logged + telemetry Fail action; mark node Failed; do not cache
Malicious action writing outside declared outputs Privilege / integrity Sandbox restricts write paths to declared output dir + tmpdir; verify post-exec that only declared paths changed UndeclaredOutputWrite violation Abort action; clean up tmp; alert operator
Resource exhaustion — unbounded parallelism DoS ibg-sched enforces --jobs + --ram-limit; back-pressure on ready-queue Queue depth metric sched.ready_queue_depth Reduce job slots; operator tunable at runtime
Resource exhaustion — graph explosion (fan-out) DoS Max node count configurable (graph.max_nodes); reject graph construction above limit IbgError::GraphTooLarge Fail build request; surface to operator
Large action output fills CAS disk Disk exhaustion CAS stores with size quota; GC sweep at high-watermark (90 % capacity) cas.disk_used_bytes alert at 85 % Trigger GC; operator increases quota or prunes
Corrupt persist DB (partial write) Data integrity Write-ahead log + CRC32 per record; epoch tags; fallback: drop state, full rebuild CRC mismatch at open → PersistError::Corrupt telemetry Truncate to last good epoch; rebuild dirty nodes
Privilege escalation via action spawn OS security Actions run as build user; no setuid bits; sandbox drops ambient capabilities seccomp policy violation logged Kill action process; alert; mark node Failed
Supply-chain — malicious tool in PATH Hermeticity Tool digest included in ActionKey; tool path in declared inputs; sandbox intercepts unexpected tool access Digest mismatch on tool file triggers rebuild Re-fetch known-good tool; invalidate affected nodes
Operator mistake — manual DB edit Integrity Checksums reject edited records PersistError::CrcMismatch on load Discard DB; cold rebuild
Remote cache poisoning (MITM) Correctness All fetched blobs verified against their Digest before materialization IbgError::DigestMismatch Drop blob; re-exec locally; alert
Watch-event storm (inotify flood) Stability Debounce window (default 50 ms); max-events-per-second cap; coalesce by path prefix watch.events_dropped_total counter Increase debounce; operator adjustable
Memory-corruption via unsafe in ibg-sandbox Safety Minimize unsafe blocks; audit with Miri; fuzz with cargo-fuzz ASAN in CI Patch + release; users rebuild with new binary
BLAKE3 computation DoS (huge file) CPU exhaustion Stat fast-path skips hash when mtime + size unchanged; streaming hash with budget limit fingerprint.hash_duration_p99 metric Operator sets max_file_hash_size; skip + warn

Sandbox Implementation Notes

  • Linux: seccomp-BPF filter installed before exec; openat / read on undeclared paths → EPERM; write outside declared output subtree → EPERM.
  • macOS: sandbox-exec with generated Scheme profile; declared inputs listed as (allow file-read-data (literal "/abs/path")).
  • Windows: Job Objects + restricted token; undeclared writes → ACCESS_DENIED.
  • Fallback mode (no seccomp support): path-auditing via LD_PRELOAD shim; weaker but still catches most violations.
  • Reproducibility check: re-run action with same ActionKey on second executor; compare output Digest; non-determinism → WARN + do not cache.
  • Tool canonicalization: resolve tool via which at graph-build time; record absolute path + Digest in ActionKey; PATH not inherited by action.

Threat-Reduction Invariants

  • Every remote blob fetched → Digest recomputed locally before materialization; mismatch → immediate error, no FS write.
  • Dynamic dep registration: always cycle-check before accepting; cycle → IbgError::DynamicCycleDetected, not a deadlock.
  • All user-supplied strings (NodeKey, command, env vars) pass through a validation pass: UTF-8, length ≤ 64 KiB, no embedded NUL.
  • Persist DB: writes via WAL with CRC32c per entry; checkpoint atomically via rename(2); torn write detected on next open.
  • No ambient authority: action processes inherit a minimal env (PATH, declared vars only); home dir not readable unless declared.

Telemetry for Security Events

  • security.sandbox_violations_total — by node_id, violation_kind.
  • security.undeclared_input_paths — emitted as structured log (path, action_key).
  • security.digest_mismatch_total — remote fetch vs local compute.
  • security.cycle_rejected_total — dynamic dep cycles.
  • security.reproducibility_failures_total — non-deterministic action detected.
  • security.tool_digest_mismatches_total — tool hash changed since last build.
  • All security events: severity ERROR, include action_key, node_id, invocation_id; forwarded to SIEM via structured log sink.

Failure Modes & Recovery

Taxonomy

F1 — Graph Construction Failures

Symptom Root Cause Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery
IbgError::CycleDetected at build start Static cycle in declared deps DFS on graph build None; caller must fix DAG Remove offending edge from BUILD definition
IbgError::GraphTooLarge Node count exceeds graph.max_nodes Count check at insert None Split graph; increase limit
Duplicate NodeKey → node shadowing BUILD DSL emits same key twice Key uniqueness index in ibg-graph Reject second insert; error Deduplicate in BUILD definition
Missing declared output from upstream Source node path does not exist Pre-eval path check in ibg-fingerprint Mark node Dirty; try rebuild Add file to workspace or fix declared path

F2 — Fingerprint / Change-Detection Failures

Symptom Root Cause Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery
Stale cache hit (under-build) Undeclared input not hashed Sandbox catches at exec time Fail build, not silent Add missing input to declaration
Excessive rebuilds (over-build) mtime changes without content change Stat fast-path masked by mtime-only compare Verify: enable --content-hash-always for diagnosis Check FS for mtime-bumping tools (rsync, backup)
Hash deadlock on circular symlink Symlink loop in workspace Depth limit (32) in path traversal Return IbgError::SymlinkLoop; skip file Fix workspace symlinks
Digest DB stale after FS corruption FS repair altered content without notifying watch ibg-watch polling fallback detects on next interval Polling re-hashes file Force --invalidate-all; cold build

F3 — Engine / Incremental Evaluation Failures

Symptom Root Cause Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery
Node stuck in Running state Hung action process; scheduler never heard exit Deadline timer per node (default 10 min) Kill process; mark Failed Check action_key in logs; fix action
Revision counter overflow >2^64 incremental builds (theoretical) Revision(u64) wrap detection None expected in practice Rotate persist DB; reset revision
Dynamic dep cycle at runtime Action discovers dep creating a cycle Cycle check on DynamicDep registration Reject dep; fail node Refactor action to avoid circular dependency
Early-cutoff missed (spurious rebuild) Output Digest comparison bug Integration test: known-no-change build must do 0 execs None File bug; pin binary version
Memoization cache eviction under memory pressure LRU in-process memo store evicts live entries engine.memo_evictions_total counter spike Re-evaluate evicted nodes Increase --memo-cache-mb

F4 — Action Cache / CAS Failures

Symptom Root Cause Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery
CAS object missing (evicted) after cache hit GC removed object between hit-check and materialize Atomic read-pin before GC; retry on CasMiss Retry exec (1 attempt) Tune GC watermark higher
ActionKey collision (hash collision) Collision in 256-bit BLAKE3 (negligible probability) Content-length mismatch check Evict entry; re-exec Investigate inputs; report
Corrupt CAS object (bit-rot) Disk error Verify Digest on read Re-exec; rewrite CAS Run fsck-equivalent; replace disk
CAS disk full Unbounded growth cas.disk_used_bytes at quota GC sweep triggered Increase quota; prune old entries
Remote cache unavailable Network partition / backend down gRPC error on fetch/push Fall back to local exec (never block build) Check remote endpoint; operator restores

F5 — Scheduler / Execution Failures

Symptom Root Cause Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery
